# SX-WSA1R GUI menu map

Generated by `wsa1/scripts/analysis/gui_menu_map.py` from the sources; do not edit by hand.
Regenerate with `make -C wsa1 gui-menu-map`; `make -C wsa1 gui-menu-map-check` fails when it is stale.

Every screen is an object in `PanelScreen_VtableTable` (prom_a): three directory slots, its Enter,
Leave and Button methods. Entries 0-47 are the panel modes, and entry 32 + n is screen id n, the
number a screen request carries. For each one this lists what the code says it draws (the display
lists its Enter and Button methods name, with their first texts), the key map its Button method
reads (the handler per panel key; do-nothing keys left out), and the screen requests those
handlers write. It is a static reading of the sources (the script's docstring says what it
follows), so a request made through a register or a list chosen through a pointer table is
missing; nothing here is more than the code states.

Record macros: `wsa1/include/wsa1_gui.inc`. Key names: the `KEY_*` equates there.

## mode 0x01: `ModeEnter_Sound`

Object `T_ModeEnter_Sound` (`PanelScreen_VtableTable` entry 1). Enter `ModeEnter_Sound`, Leave `T_F41504_Nop`, Button `ModeEnter_Combination`.

## mode 0x02: `ModeEnter_Combination`

Object `T_ModeEnter_Combination` (`PanelScreen_VtableTable` entry 2). Enter `ModeEnter_Combination`, Leave `T_F4150C_Nop`, Button `ScreenEnter_PowerOnSplash`.

## mode 0x03: `ModeEnter_Sequencer`

Object `T_ModeEnter_Sequencer` (`PanelScreen_VtableTable` entry 3). Enter `ModeEnter_Sequencer`, Leave `sub_FE8116`, Button `Paint_Sequencer`.

Draws:

- `DisplayList_SequencerRealtimeEdit`: "SEQUENCER"; "REALTIME"; "EDIT"; "MASTER"
- `DisplayList_NoteEditTrackSong`: "NOTE EDIT"; "TRACK"; "SONG"; "PLAY"
- `Sequencer_DrawTitleIcon_DL`
- `Sequencer_DrawRealtimeIcon_DL`
- `Sequencer_DrawMasterRecordIcon_DL`
- `Sequencer_DrawStepIcon_DL`
- `Sequencer_DrawSongSelectIcon_DL1`
- `Sequencer_DrawSongSelectIcon_DL2`
- `Sequencer_DrawEditIcon_DL`
- `Sequencer_DrawTrackAssignIcon_DL`
- `Sequencer_DrawMedleyIcon_DL`
- `Sequencer_DrawAfterTouchSetIcon_DL`
- `NoteEditPartSelect_DrawTitleIcon_DL`
- `DrumEditPartSelect_DrawTitleIcon_DL`

## mode 0x05: `ModeEnter_RealtimeRecord_Fwd`

## mode 0x06: `ModeEnter_StepRecord`

Object `T_ModeEnter_StepRecord` (`PanelScreen_VtableTable` entry 6). Enter `ModeEnter_StepRecord`, Leave `sub_F7D025`, Button `ScreenEnter_Edit`.
